#宗法制度
zongfazhi = { 
	potential = { 
		idea = grand_navy
	}
	trigger = { 
		has_country_flag = zongfazhidu
		NOT = { 
			legitimacy = 0.5
		}
	}
	legitimacy = 0.04
	icon = 3
}

#天命所归
the_mandate_of_heaven = { 
	potential = { 
		idea = superior_seamanship
		OR = {
			government = celestial_empire
			government = feudal_empire
		}
	}
	trigger = { 
		stability = 0
		legitimacy = 0.75
	}
	stability_investment = 15
	stability_cost_modifier = -0.1
	global_revolt_risk = -2
	global_tax_modifier = 0.08
	production_efficiency = 0.02
	icon = 3
}

#威震华夏

		legitimacy = 0.5
	}

	global_tax_modifier = 0.10
	war_exhaustion = -0.02
	icon = 3
}

#逐鹿中原
zhuluzhongyuan1 = { 
	potential = { 
		idea = naval_fighting_instruction
		num_of_cities = 8
		NOT = { num_of_cities = 16 }
	}
	trigger = { 
		any_owned_province = { 
			hre = yes
			NOT = { 
				is_core = THIS
			}
		}
		num_of_cities = 8
		NOT = { num_of_cities = 16 }
	}
	badboy = -0.15
	icon = 13
}

zhuluzhongyuan2 = { 
	potential = {

			}

#过度集权#
############################################
guodujiquan = { 
	potential = { 
		ai = no
		not = { has_country_flag = fajiazhuanjing }
	}
	trigger = { 
		or = { 
			and = { 
				not = { 
					idea = national_bank
				} # 郡县制度
				or = { 
					and = { 
						not = { 
							ADM = 7
						}
						not = { 
							centralization_decentralization = -4
						}
					}
					and = { 
						not = { 
							ADM = 6
						}
						not = { 
							centralization_decentralization = -3
						}
					}
					and = { 
						not = { 
							ADM = 5
						}
						not = { 
							centralization_decentralization = -2
						}
					}
					and = { 
						not = { 
							ADM = 4
						}
						not = { 
							centralization_decentralization = -1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			and = { 
				idea = national_bank # 郡县制度
				or = { 
					and = { 
						not = { 
							ADM = 5
						}
						not = { 
							centralization_decentralization = -4
						}
					}
					and = {

							ADM = 4
						}
						not = { 
							centralization_decentralization = -3
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	global_revolt_risk = 2 # 全国叛乱风险+2
	stability_cost_modifier = 0.2 # 全国稳定花费+20%
	icon = 4
}

#少量进口粮食—和平#
####################
shaoliangliangshi_one = { 
	potential = { 
		num_of_cities = 10
		war = no
		NOT = { 
			has_country_flag = nanxiyu
		}
	}
	trigger = { 
		OR = { 
			AND = { 
				num_of_cities = 10
				not = { 
					num_of_cities = 20
				}
				check_variable = {
					which = liangshishuliang
					value = 1
				}
				not = { 
					check_variable = {
						which = liangshishuliang
						value = 2
					}
				}
			}
			AND = { 
				num_of_cities = 20
				not = { 
					num_of_cities = 30
				}
				check_variable = {
					which = liangshishuliang
					value = 2
				}
				not = { 
					check_variable = {
						which = liangshishuliang
						value = 3
					}
				}
			}
			AND = { 
				num_of_cities = 30
				not = { 
					num_of_cities = 40
				}
				check_variable = {
					which = liangshishuliang
					value = 2
				}
				not = { 
					check_variable = {
						which = liangshishuliang
						value = 4
					}
				}
			}
			AND = { 
				num_of_cities = 40
				not = { 
					num_of_cities = 60
				}
				check_variable = {
					which = liangshishuliang
					value = 3
				}
				not = { 
					check_variable = {
						which = liangshishuliang
						value = 5
					}
				}
			}
			AND = { 
				num_of_cities = 60
				not = { 
					num_of_cities = 80
				}
				check_variable = {
					which = liangshishuliang
					value = 4
				}
				not = { 
					check_variable = {
						which = liangshishuliang
						value = 7
					}
				}
			}
			AND = { 
				num_of_cities = 80
				not = { 
					num_of_cities = 100
				}
				check_variable = {
					which = liangshishuliang
					value = 5
				}
				not = { 
					check_variable = {
						which = liangshishuliang
						value = 8
					}
				}
			}
			AND = { 
				num_of_cities = 100
				not = { 
					num_of_cities = 120
				}
				check_variable = {
					which = liangshishuliang
					value = 6
				}
				not = { 
					check_variable = {
						which = liangshishuliang
						value = 11
					}
				}
			}
			AND = { 
				num_of_cities = 120
				not = { 
					num_of_cities = 150
				}
				check_variable = {
					which = liangshishuliang
					value = 7
				}
				not = { 
					check_variable = {
						which = liangshishuliang
						value = 13
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	land_maintenance_modifier = 0.15
	stability_cost_modifier = 0.15
	global_revolt_risk = 0.5
	icon = 6
}

QIN_gift = { 
	potential = { 
		tag = QIN
		
	}
	trigger = { 
		NOT =  {  has_global_flag = zhanguokaishi  }
		any_country = { 
			OR = { 
				government = tribal_federation
				government = tribal_despotism
			}
			primary_culture = xirong
			war_with = QIN
